# R 開頭詞條

> 本檔收錄 3,553 條含中文對譯的詞目。

<a id="entry-r-000001"></a>
## r

*n.* 1. **倫琴** — a unit of radiation exposure; the dose of ionizing radiation that will produce 1 electrostatic unit of electricity in 1 cc of dry air
2. **氣體常數, 通用氣體常數** — (physics) the universal constant in the gas equation: pressure times volume = R times temperature; equal to 8.3143 joules per kelvin per mole
3. the 18th letter of the Roman alphabet
4. the length of a line segment between the center and circumference of a circle or sphere

<a id="entry-r-000002"></a>
## R&D

*n.* 1. *(abbreviation, alt-of, initialism)* **研發 /研發** — Initialism of research and development, a department within a company (or governmental division) dedicated to that task, or the fiscal category that represents it in fiscal reporting.

<a id="entry-r-000005"></a>
## ra

*n.* 1. **鐳** — an intensely radioactive metallic element that occurs in minute amounts in uranium ores
2. ancient Egyptian sun god with the head of a hawk; a universal creator; he merged with the god Amen as Amen-Ra to become the king of the gods
3. (astronomy) the equatorial coordinate specifying the angle, measured eastward along the celestial equator, from the vernal equinox to the intersection of the hour circle that passes through an object in the sky; usually expressed in hours and minutes and seconds; used with declination to specify positions on the celestial sphere
*“one hour of right ascension equals fifteen degrees”*

## rabbit

**發音：** /ˈɹæbɪt/
*n.* 1. **兔, 兔子** — any of various burrowing animals of the family Leporidae having long ears and short tails; some domesticated and raised for pets or food
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **兔子, 白兔** — A mammal of most genera of the family Leporidae, with long ears, long hind legs and a short, fluffy tail. (Those of the larger kind are instead called hares)
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **兔子** — A runner in a distance race whose goal is mainly to set the pace, either to tire a specific rival so that a teammate can win or to help another break a record; a pacesetter.
4. **兔毛, 兔的毛皮, 兔皮** — the fur of a rabbit
5. flesh of any of various rabbits or hares (wild or domesticated) eaten as food
*v.* 1. hunt rabbits

3. an uncooked vegetable

<a id="entry-r-000027"></a>
## race

**發音：** /ɹeɪs/
*v.* 1. **快進, 趕快, 趕緊, 飛馳, 衝進** — move fast
*“He rushed down the hall to receive his guests”*
*“The cars raced down the street”*
2. *(intransitive)* **飛馳 /飛馳, 疾馳 /疾馳** — To move or drive at high speed; to hurry or speed.
3. **使急速移動** — cause to move fast or to rush or race
*“The psychologist raced the rats through a long maze”*
4. compete in a race
*“he is running the Marathon this year”*
*“let's race and see who gets there first”*
5. to work as fast as possible towards a goal, sometimes in competition with others
*“We are racing to find a cure for AIDS”*
*n.* 1. **競賽, 比賽** — a contest of speed
*“the race is to the swift”*
2. **人種, 種族** — people who are believed to belong to the same genetic stock
*“some biologists doubt that there are important genetic differences between races of human beings”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **比賽 /比賽, 競賽 /競賽** — A contest between people, animals, vehicles, etc. where the goal is to be the first to reach some objective.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **種 /種, 種族 /種族** — A large group of people distinguished from others on the basis of a common heritage.
5. any competition
*“the race for the presidency”*
6. (biology) a taxonomic group that is a division of a species; usually arises as a consequence of geographical isolation within a species

## racial

*“racial differences”*
*“racial discrimination”*

*n.* 1. **種族免疫** — natural immunity shared by all members of a particular race

<a id="entry-r-000053"></a>
## racial profiling

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **種族歸納 /種族歸納, 種族貌相 /種族貌相, 種族臉譜化 /種族臉譜化, 種族定性 /種族定性** — The practice (usually considered discreditable), by some police and other officials, of treating members of certain racial groups as more likely to be involved in criminal or other antisocial behavior than members of other racial groups.
2. a form of racism consisting of the (alleged) policy of policemen who stop and search vehicles driven by persons belonging to particular racial groups

2. *(uncountable, usually)* **種族主義 /種族主義, 種族歧視 /種族歧視** — Prejudice or discrimination based upon race or ethnicity; (countable) an action of such discrimination.

## racist

**發音：** /ˈɹeɪ.sɪst/
*n.* 1. **種族主義者 /種族主義者, 種族歧視者 /種族歧視者** — A person who believes in or supports racism; a person who believes that a particular race is superior to others, or who discriminates against other races.
2. a person with a prejudiced belief that one race is superior to others
*adj.* 1. **種族歧視的 /種族歧視的** — Constituting, exhibiting, advocating, or pertaining to racism.
2. based on racial intolerance

<a id="entry-r-000061"></a>
## rack

**發音：** /ɹæk/
*n.* 1. **架子, 支架** — framework for holding objects
2. **架子** — A series of one or more shelves, stacked one above the other.
3. **架** — Any of various kinds of frame for holding luggage or other objects on a vehicle or vessel.
4. *(historical)* **刑架** — A device, incorporating a ratchet, used to torture victims by stretching them beyond their natural limits.
5. rib section of a forequarter of veal or pork or especially lamb or mutton
6. the destruction or collapse of something
*“wrack and ruin”*
*v.* 1. **小步跑, 快步跑** — go at a rack
*“the horses single-footed”*
2. **在架上製作, 放在架上** — put on a rack and pinion
*“rack a camera”*
3. **勒索, 敲詐** — obtain by coercion or intimidation
*“They extorted money from the executive by threatening to reveal his past to the company boss”*
*“They squeezed money from the owner of the business by threatening him”*
4. **順風疾駛, 順風行駛** — run before a gale
5. **冒著大風飛行** — fly in high wind
6. stretch to the limits
*“rack one's brains”*

## racket

**發音：** /ˈɹækɪt/
*n.* 1. *(countable)* **球拍** — An implement with a handle connected to a round frame strung with wire, sinew, or plastic cords, and used to hit a ball, such as in tennis or a shuttlecock in badminton.
2. **喧吵** — A loud noise.
3. a loud and disturbing noise
4. an illegal enterprise (such as extortion or fraud or drug peddling or prostitution) carried on for profit
5. the auditory experience of sound that lacks musical quality; sound that is a disagreeable auditory experience
*“modern music is just noise to me”*
6. a sports implement (usually consisting of a handle and an oval frame with a tightly interlaced network of strings) used to strike a ball (or shuttlecock) in various games
*v.* 1. **作樂, 狂歡, 狂歡作樂** — celebrate noisily, often indulging in drinking; engage in uproarious festivities
*“The members of the wedding party made merry all night”*
*“Let's whoop it up--the boss is gone!”*
2. **喧譁, 大聲吵鬧, 吵鬧, 喧鬧** — make loud and annoying noises
3. hit (a ball) with a racket

## radar

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **雷達 /雷達** — In full primary radar: a method of detecting a distant object and determining its position, velocity, or other characteristics by analysing radio waves (usually microwaves) which are sent towards the object and which reflect off its surfaces; also, the field of study of this method.

*v.* 1. twist or braid together, interlace

<a id="entry-r-000080"></a>
## raddled

*adj.* 1. **不能再用+的, 壞掉+的, 磨破+的, 穿舊+的** — used until no longer useful
*“battered trumpets and raddled radios”*
*“worn-out shoes with flapping soles”*
2. **疲倦+的** — showing the wearing effects of overwork or care or suffering
*“looking careworn as she bent over her mending”*
*“her face was drawn and haggard from sleeplessness”*

<a id="entry-r-000081"></a>
## radfem

*n.* 1. *(derogatory, slang, sometimes)* **激女** — A radical feminist.

<a id="entry-r-000082"></a>
## radial

*adj.* 1. **橈骨+的** — relating to or near the radius
*“the radial aspect of the forearm”*
2. **徑向+的** — relating to or moving along or having the direction of a radius
*“radial velocity”*
3. **光線+的** — issuing in rays from a common center; relating to rays of light
*“radial heat”*
4. **放射狀+的** — arranged like rays or radii; radiating from a common center
*“radial symmetry”*
*“a starlike or stellate arrangement of petals”*
*n.* 1. pneumatic tire that has radial-ply casing

<a id="entry-r-000083"></a>
## radial pulse

*n.* 1. **橈動脈脈搏** — pulse of the radial artery (felt in the wrist)

<a id="entry-r-000084"></a>
## radial velocity

*n.* 1. **徑向速度** — velocity along the line of sight toward or away from the observer

<a id="entry-r-000085"></a>
## radial-ply

*adj.* 1. **輻射層+的** — of or relating to automobile tires that have a strip under the tread and relatively little stiffening in the sidewalls
*“radial-ply tires”*

<a id="entry-r-000086"></a>
## radially

*adv.* 1. **放射狀+地, 輻射+地** — in a radial manner
*“an imaginative dispersal of the pews radially from the central focus of the pulpit”*

<a id="entry-r-000087"></a>
## radially symmetrical

*adj.* 1. **中心對稱+的** — having a symmetrical arrangement of radiating parts about a central point

<a id="entry-r-000088"></a>
## radian

**發音：** /ˈɹeɪdiən/
*n.* 1. **弧度** — In the International System of Units, the derived unit of plane angle: the angle subtended at the centre of a circle by an arc of its circumference equal in length to the radius of the circle.
2. **弧度** — the unit of plane angle adopted under the Systeme International d'Unites; equal to the angle at the center of a circle subtended by an arc equal in length to the radius (approximately 57.295 degrees)

<a id="entry-r-000089"></a>
## radiance

**發音：** /ˈɹeɪdi.əns/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **輻射 /輻射, 光輝 /光輝, 閃耀 /閃耀** — The quality or state of being radiant; shining, bright or splendid.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **輻射亮度 /輻射亮度, 輻亮度 /輻亮度, 輻射率 /輻射率** — The flux of radiation emitted per unit solid angle in a given direction by a unit area of a source.
3. **容光煥發** — an attractive combination of good health and happiness
*“the radiance of her countenance”*
4. the amount of electromagnetic radiation leaving or arriving at a point on a surface
5. the quality of being bright and sending out rays of light

<a id="entry-r-000090"></a>
## radiant flux

*n.* 1. **輻射通量** — the rate of flow of radiant energy (electromagnetic waves)

<a id="entry-r-000091"></a>
## radiant heating

*n.* 1. **輻射供暖, 輻射加熱** — heating a building by radiation from panels containing hot water or electrical heaters

<a id="entry-r-000092"></a>
## radiantly

*adv.* 1. **容光煥發的, 清朗+地, 燦爛的, 輝煌+地** — in a radiant manner
*“the bride smiled radiantly”*

<a id="entry-r-000093"></a>
## radiate

*v.* 1. **發出熱量, 發光, 輻射** — send out rays or waves
*“The sun radiates heat”*
2. **輻射, 放射, 傳播, 散發** — extend or spread outward from a center or focus or inward towards a center
*“spokes radiate from the hub of the wheel”*
*“This plants radiate spines in all directions”*
3. **發紅, 使光亮, 容光煥發, 閃亮, 發熱** — have a complexion with a strong bright color, such as red or pink
*“Her face glowed when she came out of the sauna”*
4. **散發, 發光** — cause to be seen by emitting light as if in rays
*“The sun is radiating”*
5. **多樣化** — spread into new habitats and produce variety or variegate
*“The plants on this island diversified”*
6. send out real or metaphoric rays
*“She radiates happiness”*
*adj.* 1. **放射狀+的** — arranged like rays or radii; radiating from a common center
*“radial symmetry”*
*“a starlike or stellate arrangement of petals”*
2. having rays or ray-like parts as in the flower heads of daisies

<a id="entry-r-000094"></a>
## radiation

**發音：** /ˌɹeɪ.diˈeɪ.ʃən/
*n.* 1. **放射, 放射性, 放射現象** — the spontaneous emission of a stream of particles or electromagnetic rays in nuclear decay
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **放射, 輻射 /輻射, 放射性能量** — Radioactive energy.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **輻射 /輻射** — The process of radiating waves or particles.
4. **放射病** — syndrome resulting from exposure to ionizing radiation (e.g., exposure to radioactive chemicals or to nuclear explosions); low doses cause diarrhea and nausea and vomiting and sometimes loss of hair; greater exposure can cause sterility and cataracts and some forms of cancer and other diseases; severe exposure can cause death within hours
*“he was suffering from radiation”*
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **輻射 /輻射** — The transfer of energy via radiation.
6. *(countable)* **輻射演化 /輻射演化, 適應輻射 /適應輻射** — A rapid diversification of an ancestral species into many new forms.

<a id="entry-r-000095"></a>
## radiation sickness

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **輻射病 /輻射病, 放射病** — Any illness produced by ionizing radiation with symptoms ranging from nausea to death.
2. **放射病** — syndrome resulting from exposure to ionizing radiation (e.g., exposure to radioactive chemicals or to nuclear explosions); low doses cause diarrhea and nausea and vomiting and sometimes loss of hair; greater exposure can cause sterility and cataracts and some forms of cancer and other diseases; severe exposure can cause death within hours
*“he was suffering from radiation”*

<a id="entry-r-000096"></a>
## radiation syndrome

*n.* 1. **放射病** — syndrome resulting from exposure to ionizing radiation (e.g., exposure to radioactive chemicals or to nuclear explosions); low doses cause diarrhea and nausea and vomiting and sometimes loss of hair; greater exposure can cause sterility and cataracts and some forms of cancer and other diseases; severe exposure can cause death within hours
*“he was suffering from radiation”*

<a id="entry-r-000097"></a>
## radiator

**發音：** /ˈrædɪeɪ̯tə/
*n.* 1. **散熱器 /散熱器** — A device that lowers engine coolant temperature by conducting heat to the air, through metal fins.
2. **暖氣 /暖氣** — A finned metal fixture that carries hot water or steam in order to heat a room.
3. any object that radiates energy
4. heater consisting of a series of pipes for circulating steam or hot water to heat rooms or buildings
5. a mechanism consisting of a metal honeycomb through which hot fluids circulate; heat is transferred from the fluid through the honeycomb to the airstream that is created either by the motion of the vehicle or by a fan

<a id="entry-r-000098"></a>
## radiator grille

*n.* 1. **散熱器格柵** — grating that admits cooling air to car's radiator

<a id="entry-r-000099"></a>
## radical

**發音：** /ˈɹædɪkəl/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **根的, 根部的** — Of or pertaining to the root of a plant.
2. *(not-comparable)* **詞根的 /詞根的** — Of or pertaining to the root of a word.
3. **詞根+的** — of or relating to or constituting a linguistic root
*“a radical verb form”*
4. **根本的** — Pertaining to the basic or intrinsic nature of something.
5. (used of opinions and actions) far beyond the norm
*“extremist political views”*
*“radical opinions on education”*
6. markedly new or introducing radical change
*“a revolutionary discovery”*
*“radical political views”*
*n.* 1. **化學族** — (chemistry) two or more atoms bound together as a single unit and forming part of a molecule
2. **激進分子, 激進組織** — an atom or group of atoms with at least one unpaired electron; in the body it is usually an oxygen molecule that has lost an electron and will stabilize itself by stealing an electron from a nearby molecule
*“in the body free radicals are high-energy particles that ricochet wildly and damage cells”*
3. **激進分子** — a person who has radical ideas or opinions
4. **根** — A root (of a number or quantity).
5. **部首** — In logographic writing systems such as the Chinese writing system, the portion of a character (if any) that provides an indication of its meaning, as opposed to being wholly phonetic.
6. **詞根, 部首, 偏旁** — In Semitic languages, any one of the set of consonants (typically three) that make up a root.

## radical feminism

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **激進女權主義** — A type of feminism which calls for a radical reordering of society to eliminate male supremacy in all social and economic contexts.

<a id="entry-r-000101"></a>
## radicalism

**發音：** /ˈradɪkəlɪz(ə)m/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **激進主義 /激進主義, 急進主義 /急進主義** — Any of various radical social or political movements that aim at fundamental change in the structure of society
2. the political orientation of those who favor revolutionary change in government and society

<a id="entry-r-000125"></a>
## radioactivity

**發音：** /ˌreɪdɪəʊakˈtɪvɪti/

2. **放射, 放射性, 放射現象** — the spontaneous emission of a stream of particles or electromagnetic rays in nuclear decay
3. *(uncountable, usually)* **放射, 放射線 /放射線** — The radiation so emitted; including gamma rays, alpha particles, neutrons, electrons, positrons, etc.

*“barium sulfate is radiopaque”*

<a id="entry-r-000145"></a>

## radiosensitive

<a id="entry-r-000156"></a>
## radius

**發音：** /ˈɹeɪ.di.əs/
*n.* 1. **半徑** — a straight line from the center to the perimeter of a circle (or from the center to the surface of a sphere)
2. **橈骨 /橈骨** — The lighter bone (or fused portion of bone) in the forelimb of an animal.
3. **半徑 /半徑** — The length of this line segment.

*“rag ore”*

<a id="entry-r-000325"></a>

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **隨機存取存儲器 /隨機存取存儲器, 隨機存儲器 /隨機存儲器, 隨機存取記憶體 /隨機存取記憶體, 暫存記憶體 /暫存記憶體** — Computer memory, usually volatile, that stores program and data values during operation and in which each word of memory may be directly (randomly) accessed.

## rate

*n.* 1. **率** — a magnitude or frequency relative to a time unit
*“they traveled at a rate of 55 miles per hour”*
*“the rate of change was faster than expected”*
2. **費率** — amount of a charge or payment relative to some basis
*“a 10-minute phone call at that rate would cost $5”*
3. **速率, 速度** — the relative speed of progress or change
*“he lived at a fast pace”*
*“he works at a great rate”*
4. a quantity or amount or measure considered as a proportion of another quantity or amount or measure
*“the literacy rate”*
*“the retention rate”*
*v.* 1. **排名, 評價, 評估** — assign a rank or rating to
*“how would you rank these students?”*
*“The restaurant is rated highly in the food guide”*
2. **估價, 比率** — be worthy of or have a certain rating
*“This bond rates highly”*
3. estimate the value of

*“Gold was rated highly among the Romans”*

## rather

**發音：** /ˈɹɑː.ðə(ɹ)/
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **比較 /比較, 與其 /與其, 寧願 /寧願** — Used to specify a choice or preference; preferably, in preference to. (Now usually followed by than)
2. *(conjunctive, not-comparable)* **反而, 反倒** — Used to introduce a contradiction; on the contrary.
3. *(not-comparable)* **相當 /相當** — Somewhat, quite; to an unexpected degree.
4. on the contrary
*“rather than disappoint the children, he did two quick tricks before he left”*
*“he didn't call; rather (or instead), he wrote her a letter"”*
5. to some (great or small) extent
*“it was rather cold”*

6. more readily or willingly
*“clean it well, preferably with warm water”*

## rational

**發音：** /ˈɹæʃ(ə)nəl/
*adj.* 1. **理性+的, 合理+的** — consistent with or based on or using reason
*“rational behavior”*
*“a process of rational inference”*
2. **合理的** — Logically sound; not self-contradictory or otherwise absurd.
3. **有理數+的** — capable of being expressed as a quotient of integers
*“rational numbers”*
4. *(not-comparable)* **有理** — Of a number, capable of being expressed as the ratio of two integers.
5. of or associated with or requiring the use of the mind
*“intellectual problems”*
*“the triumph of the rational over the animal side of man”*
6. having its source in or being guided by the intellect (as distinguished from experience or emotion)
*“a rational analysis”*
*n.* 1. **有理數** — an integer or a fraction

<a id="entry-r-000463"></a>
## rationale

**發音：** /ˌɹæʃ.əˈnɑːl/
*n.* 1. **推理, 原理** — An explanation of the basis or fundamental reasons for something.
2. **理由** — A justification or rationalization for something.
3. *(rare)* **主教法衣** — A liturgical vestment worn by some Christian bishops of various denominations.
4. (law) an explanation of the fundamental reasons (especially an explanation of the working of some device in terms of laws of nature)
*“the rationale for capital punishment”*
*“the principles of internal-combustion engines”*

<a id="entry-r-000464"></a>
## rationalisation

*n.* 1. **文飾作用** — (psychiatry) a defense mechanism by which your true motivation is concealed by explaining your actions and feelings in a way that is not threatening
2. the cognitive process of making something seem consistent with or based on reason
3. (mathematics) the simplification of an expression or equation by eliminating radicals without changing the value of the expression or the roots of the equation
4. the organization of a business according to scientific principles of management in order to increase efficiency
5. systematic organization; the act of organizing something according to a system or a rationale

<a id="entry-r-000465"></a>
## rationalise

*v.* 1. **使合理, 合理地處理** — structure and run according to rational or scientific principles in order to achieve desired results
*“We rationalized the factory's production and raised profits”*
2. **辯解, 辯護** — defend, explain, clear away, or make excuses for by reasoning
*“rationalize the child's seemingly crazy behavior”*
*“he rationalized his lack of success”*
3. think rationally; employ logic or reason
*“When one wonders why one is doing certain things, one should rationalize”*
4. remove irrational quantities from
*“This function can be rationalized”*
5. weed out unwanted or unnecessary things
*“We had to lose weight, so we cut the sugar from our diet”*

5. **射線 /射線, 光線 /光線** — A beam of light or radiation.
6. **鱝 /鱝, 鰩 /鰩, 魟魚 /魟魚** — Any of the superorder Batoidea of marine fish with flat bodies, large wing-like fins, and whip-like tails.
*v.* 1. **射出光線, 放射光線** — emit as rays
*“That tower rays a laser beam for miles across the sky”*

## reach

**發音：** /ɹiːt͡ʃ/
*v.* 1. **到達, 抵達, 達到** — reach a destination, either real or abstract
*“We hit Detroit by noon”*
*“The water reached the doorstep”*
2. **達到** — reach a point in time, or a certain state or level
*“The thermometer hit 100 degrees”*
*“This car can reach a speed of 140 miles per hour”*
3. **實現, 取得, 完成, 達到** — to gain with effort
*“she achieved her goal despite setbacks”*
4. **伸出, 達到** — to extend as far as
*“The sunlight reached the wall”*
*“Can he reach?" "The chair must not touch the wall”*
5. **達到** — reach a goal, e.g., "make the first team"
*“We made it!”*
*“She may not make the grade”*
6. **遞給, 交給, 給, 傳遞** — place into the hands or custody of
*“hand me the spoon, please”*
*“Turn the files over to me, please”*
*n.* 1. **範圍, 界限** — an area in which something acts or operates or has power or control: "the range of a supersonic jet"
*“a piano has a greater range than the human voice”*
*“the ambit of municipal legislation”*
2. the limits within which something can be effective
*“range of motion”*
*“he was beyond the reach of their fire”*
3. the act of physically reaching or thrusting out
4. the limit of capability
*“within the compass of education”*

*“The hydrogen and the oxygen react”*

<a id="entry-r-000574"></a>
## reactant

2. **反應物 /反應物** — Any of the participants present at the start of a chemical reaction, specifically one that is consumed during the reaction.

<a id="entry-r-000575"></a>
## reaction

**發音：** /ɹiˈækʃən/
*n.* 1. **化學反應, 反應** — (chemistry) a process in which one or more substances are changed into others
*“there was a chemical reaction of the lime with the ground water”*
2. **反應 /反應, 反動 /反動** — An action or statement in response to a stimulus or other event.
3. **反應 /反應** — A transformation in which one or more substances is converted into another by combination or decomposition.
4. **反作用力** — (mechanics) the equal and opposite force that is produced when any force is applied to a body
*“every action has an equal and opposite reaction”*
5. **反應** — a response that reveals a person's feelings or attitude
*“he was pleased by the audience's reaction to his performance”*
*“John feared his mother's reaction when she saw the broken lamp”*
6. an idea evoked by some experience
*“his reaction to the news was to start planning what to do”*

<a id="entry-r-000576"></a>
## reaction formation

*n.* 1. **反應形成** — (psychiatry) a defense mechanism in which a person unconsciously develops attitudes and behavior that are the opposite of unacceptable repressed desires and impulses and serve to conceal them
*“his strict morality is just a reaction formation to hide his sexual drive”*

<a id="entry-r-000577"></a>
## reaction mechanism

*n.* 1. **反應機理 /反應機理** — the ordered set of individual reactions whose overall effect is given by a chemical equation

<a id="entry-r-000578"></a>

2. **反應時間, 潛育期, 潛伏期** — the time that elapses between a stimulus and the response to it

6. interpret something in a certain way; convey a particular meaning or impression

## ready

**發音：** /ˈɹɛd.i/
*adj.* 1. **準備好+的, 準備就緒+的** — completely prepared or in condition for immediate action or use or progress
*“get ready”*
*“she is ready to resign”*
2. **甘心+的, 樂意+的, 情願+的** — mentally disposed
*“he was ready to believe her”*
3. **準備好的 /準備好的** — Prepared for immediate action or use.
4. (of especially money) immediately available
*“he seems to have ample ready money”*
*“a ready source of cash”*
5. made suitable and available for immediate use
*“dinner is ready”*
6. apprehending and responding with speed and sensitivity
*“a quick mind”*
*“a ready wit”*
*n.* 1. **預備好的狀態** — poised for action
*“their guns were at the ready”*
*v.* 1. **做菜, 烹調, 煮, 做** — prepare for eating by applying heat
*“Cook me dinner, please”*
*“can you make me an omelette?”*
2. **使準備好, 使適合, 準備, 裝備, 起草** — make ready or suitable or equip in advance for a particular purpose or for some use, event, etc
*“Get the children ready for school!”*
*“prepare for war”*

## real

**發音：** /ɹiːl/
*adj.* 1. **實際存在+的, 真實+的, 實際+的** — being or occurring in fact or actuality; having verified existence; not illusory
*“real objects”*
*“real people; not ghosts"”*
2. **名副其實+的, 真正+的** — no less than what is stated; worthy of the name
*“the real reason”*
*“real war”*
3. **真正+的, 真實+的** — not to be taken lightly
*“statistics demonstrate that poverty and unemployment are very real problems”*
*“to the man sleeping regularly in doorways homelessness is real”*
4. **實際+的** — being or reflecting the essential or genuine character of something
*“her actual motive”*
*“a literal solitude like a desert"- G.K.Chesterton”*
5. **真的, 真實的 /真實的** — True, genuine, not merely nominal or apparent.
6. **不虛假的, 實際的, 真正的, 真的, 真實+的** — coinciding with reality
*“perceptual error...has a surprising resemblance to veridical perception"- F.A.Olafson”*
*adv.* 1. used as intensifiers; `real' is sometimes used informally for `really'; `rattling' is informal
*“she was very gifted”*
*“he played very well”*
*n.* 1. **雷亞爾 /雷亞爾** — A unit of currency used in Brazil since 1994. Symbol: R$.
2. **實數** — any rational or irrational number
3. **雷亞爾** — the basic unit of money in Brazil; equal to 100 centavos
4. **西班牙古銀幣, 雷阿爾** — an old small silver Spanish coin

## realism

**發音：** /ˈɹi.əlɪzəm/
*n.* 1. **實用主義, 現實主義** — the attribute of accepting the facts of life and favoring practicality and literal truth
2. **真實性, 現實** — the state of being actual or real
*“the reality of his situation slowly dawned on him”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **現實主義 /現實主義** — A concern for fact or reality and rejection of the impractical and visionary.
4. (philosophy) the philosophical doctrine that physical objects continue to exist when not perceived
5. an artistic movement in 19th century France; artists and writers strove for detailed realistic and factual description
6. (philosophy) the philosophical doctrine that abstract concepts exist independent of their names

<a id="entry-r-000637"></a>
## realist

**發音：** /ˈɹɪəlɪst/
*n.* 1. **現實主義者 /現實主義者, 實在論者 /實在論者** — One who believes in seeing things the way they really are, as opposed to how they would like them to be.
2. a philosopher who believes that universals are real and exist independently of anyone thinking of them
3. a person who accepts the world as it literally is and deals with it accordingly
4. a painter who represents the world realistically and not in an idealized or romantic style

<a id="entry-r-000638"></a>

*“a realistic novel”*

*“the reason that war was declared”*
*“the grounds for their declaration”*
6. the capacity for rational thought or inference or discrimination
*“we are told that man is endowed with reason and capable of distinguishing good from evil”*
*v.* 1. **爭辯, 評理, 討論, 辯論, 爭論** — present reasons and arguments

## reciprocal

**發音：** /ɹɪˈsɪpɹək(ə)l/
*adj.* 1. **彼此+的, 相互+的** — concerning each of two or more persons or things; especially given or done in return
*“reciprocal aid”*
*“reciprocal trade”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **相互, 互相, 互惠** — Of a feeling, action or such: mutual, uniformly felt or done by each party towards the other or others; two-way.
3. of or relating to the multiplicative inverse of a quantity or function
*“the reciprocal ratio of a:b is b:a”*
*n.* 1. **互惠, 相互** — something (a term or expression or concept) that has a reciprocal relation to something else
*“risk is the reciprocal of safety”*
2. **倒數** — The number obtained by dividing 1 by another given number; the result of exchanging the numerator and the denominator of a fraction.
3. **乘法逆元素** — (mathematics) one of a pair of numbers whose product is 1: the reciprocal of 2/3 is 3/2; the multiplicative inverse of 7 is 1/7
4. hybridization involving a pair of crosses that reverse the sexes associated with each genotype

*“gave up our seats on the plane and in return received several hundred dollars and seats on the next plane out”*
*“we get many benefits in return for our taxes”*
2. **互助, 互相+地, 彼此+地, 相互+地** — in a mutual or shared manner
*“the agreement was mutually satisfactory”*
*“the goals of the negotiators were not reciprocally exclusive”*
3. in an inverse or contrary manner
*“inversely related”*
*“wavelength and frequency are, of course, related reciprocally"- F.A.Geldard”*

*“The patient is recuperating”*

*“The company managed to recuperate”*
4. restore to good health or strength

3. return in thought or speech to something

## reductionism

In a reductionist framework, the phenomena that can be explained completely in terms of relations between other more fundamental phenomena are called "epiphenomena".

3. the analysis of complex things into simpler constituents

## reference

**發音：** /ˈɹɛf.(ə.)ɹəns/
*n.* 1. **引用, 語錄, 引文** — a short note recognizing a source of information or of a quoted passage
*“the student's essay failed to list several important citations”*
*“the acknowledgments are usually printed at the front of a book”*
2. **參考, 參考點** — an indicator that orients you generally
*“it is used as a reference for comparing the heating and the electrical energy involved”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **引用** — An object containing information which refers to data stored elsewhere, as opposed to containing the data itself.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **參考 /參考** — A measurement one can compare (some other measurement) to.
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **證明 /證明** — Information about a person, provided by someone (a referee) with whom they are well acquainted.
6. **參考資料, 來源** — a publication (or a passage from a publication) that is referred to
*“he carried an armful of references back to his desk”*
*“he spent hours looking for the source of that quotation”*
*v.* 1. *(proscribed, sometimes)* **引用, 提及, 提到** — To mention, to refer to.
2. refer to
*“he referenced his colleagues' work”*

4. *(countable, uncountable)* **回歸 /迴歸, 迴歸 /迴歸** — An analytic method to measure the association of one or more independent variables with a dependent variable.

6. returning to a former state

<a id="entry-r-001234"></a>
## regret

**發音：** /ɹɪˈɡɹɛt/
*v.* 1. **後悔, 懊悔** — feel remorse for; feel sorry for; be contrite about
2. **後悔 /後悔, 遺憾 /遺憾** — To feel sorry about (a thing that has or has not happened), afterthink: to wish that a thing had not happened, that something else had happened instead.
3. feel sad about the loss or absence of
4. decline formally or politely
*“I regret I can't come to the party”*
5. express with regret
*“I regret to say that you did not gain admission to Harvard”*
*n.* 1. **後悔, 懊悔, 遺憾, 悲傷** — sadness associated with some wrong done or some disappointment
*“he drank to drown his sorrows”*
*“he wrote a note expressing his regret”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **遺憾 /遺憾, 後悔 /後悔** — Emotional pain on account of something done or experienced in the past, with a wish that it had been different; a looking back with dissatisfaction or with longing.

## regular

**發音：** /ˈɹɛɡjʊlə/
*adj.* 1. **習慣性+的, 平常+的, 有規律+的, 慣例+的, 規則+的** — in accordance with fixed order or procedure or principle
*“his regular calls on his customers”*

*“a regular morass of details”*

*“at a regular meeting of the PTA”*

*“a regular beat”*
*“the even rhythm of his breathing”*
6. **常, 頻繁 /頻繁** — Having a constant pattern; showing evenness of form or appearance.
*n.* 1. **常客, 熟客** — A frequent, routine visitor to an establishment.

*“a bum who is a Central Park fixture”*
4. a soldier in the regular army

**發音：** /ˌɹiː.ɪnˈfɔːs/
*v.* 1. **增援, 補充** — strengthen and support with rewards
*“Let's reinforce good behavior”*
2. *(transitive)* **加強 /加強** — To strengthen, especially by addition or augmentation.
3. *(transitive)* **再次鼓勵, 再次強調** — To emphasize or review.
4. make stronger
*“he reinforced the concrete”*

<a id="entry-r-001304"></a>
## reinforced

*adj.* 1. **加強+的, 強化+的** — given added strength or support
*“reinforced concrete contains steel bars or metal netting”*
2. (used of soaps or cleaning agents) having a substance (an abrasive or filler) added to increase effectiveness
*“the built liquid detergents”*

<a id="entry-r-001305"></a>
## reinforced concrete

*n.* 1. **鋼筋混凝土, 鋼骨水泥** — concrete with metal and/or mesh added to provide extra support against stresses
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **鋼筋混凝土 /鋼筋混凝土** — A building material made from Portland cement concrete with a matrix of steel bars or wires (rebars) to increase its tensile strength.

<a id="entry-r-001306"></a>
## reinforcement learning

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **強化學習 /強化學習** — A type of explorative learning via feedback in a simulated environment.

<a id="entry-r-001307"></a>
## reinforcer

*n.* 1. **強化物 /強化物, 強化因素 /強化因素, 增強物 /增強物** — Something that reinforces; something that strengthens a behavior.
2. (psychology) a stimulus that strengthens or weakens the behavior that produced it

<a id="entry-r-001308"></a>
## reinstall

*v.* 1. **重灌, 重裝 /重裝** — To install again.
2. install again
*“She reinstalled the washer after it had been repaired”*

<a id="entry-r-001309"></a>
## reinstate

*v.* 1. **使復原, 使復工, 使復職, 使恢復原狀, 使恢復原職** — restore to the previous state or rank
2. **恢復** — bring back into original existence, use, function, or position
*“restore law and order”*
*“reestablish peace in the region”*

<a id="entry-r-001310"></a>
## reinstatement

*n.* 1. **復職, 復原** — the condition of being reinstated
*“her reinstatement to her former office followed quickly”*
2. **復原, 復工, 復職** — the act of restoring someone to a previous position
*“we insisted on the reinstatement of the colonel”*

<a id="entry-r-001311"></a>
## reinsurance

*n.* 1. **再保險** — sharing the risk by insurance companies; part or all of the insurer's risk is assumed by other companies in return for part of the premium paid by the insured
*“reinsurance enables a client to get coverage that would be too great for any one company to assume”*

<a id="entry-r-001312"></a>
## reinsure

*v.* 1. **再保險, 轉保** — provide additional insurance for
2. insure again by transferring to another insurance company all or a part of a liability assumed
3. insure again by assuming all or a part of the liability of an insurance company already covering a risk

<a id="entry-r-001313"></a>
## reintegrate

*v.* 1. **使重新完整, 再重新完整, 重建, 恢復** — integrate again
*“Russia must be reintegrated into Europe”*

*v.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **做無用功 /做無用功, 重新發明輪子 /重新發明輪子** — To do work unnecessarily when it has already been done satisfactorily by others; to attempt to devise a solution to a problem when a solution already exists.

*adj.* 1. **恢復精神+的** — with restored energy

*“He refused my offer of hospitality”*
2. **不許可, 不贊成, 拒絕, 不同意** — deem wrong or inappropriate
*“I disapprove of her child rearing methods”*

*n.* 1. the person or thing that is rejected or set aside as inferior in quality

<a id="entry-r-001331"></a>
## rejuvenate

**發音：** /ɹɪˈd͡ʒuːvəneɪt/
*v.* 1. **返老還童 /返老還童, 恢復活力 /恢復活力, 使變得年輕 /使變得年輕** — To render young again.
2. **使回春, 使更生** — cause (a stream or river) to erode, as by an uplift of the land
3. develop youthful topographical features
*“the land rejuvenated”*
4. make younger or more youthful
*“The contact with his grandchildren rejuvenated him”*
5. return to life; get or give new life or energy
*“The week at the spa restored me”*
6. become young again
*“The old man rejuvenated when he became a grandfather”*

<a id="entry-r-001332"></a>
## rejuvenation

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **回復 /回覆** — The process of rendering young again.
2. **回春, 復壯, 恢復活力, 返老還童** — the act of restoring to a more youthful condition
3. the phenomenon of vitality and freshness being restored
*“the annual rejuvenation of the landscape”*

## rekindle

2. **再點火, 重新燃燒, 重新點燃** — kindle anew, as of a fire
3. arouse again
*“rekindle hopes”*
*“rekindle her love”*

## relapsing fever

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **回歸熱 /迴歸熱** — An infection caused by certain bacteria of the genus Borrelia, transmitted by lice and ticks.
2. **迴歸熱** — marked by recurring high fever and transmitted by the bite of infected lice or ticks; characterized by episodes of high fever and chills and headache and muscle pain and nausea that recur every week or ten days for several months

<a id="entry-r-001335"></a>
## relate

**發音：** /ɹɪˈleɪt/
*v.* 1. **使發生聯繫, 連接, 鏈接, 連結, 聯合** — make a logical or causal connection
*“I cannot connect these two pieces of evidence in my mind”*
*“colligate these facts”*
2. **關係到, 有關, 涉及** — be relevant to
*“There were lots of questions referring to her talk”*
*“My remark pertained to your earlier comments”*
3. **相互關連, 有關** — be in a relationship with
*“How are these two observations related?”*
4. **合得來** — have or establish a relationship to
*“She relates well to her peers”*
5. *(intransitive)* **有關 /有關** — To have a connection.
6. give an account of
*“The witness related the events”*

<a id="entry-r-001336"></a>
## related

**發音：** /ɹɪˈleɪtɪd/
*adj.* 1. **有關聯+的, 有關係+的** — being connected either logically or causally or by shared characteristics
*“painting and the related arts”*
*“school-related activities”*
2. **有親緣關係+的** — connected by kinship, common origin, or marriage
3. **有關 /有關** — Standing in relation or connection.

<a id="entry-r-001337"></a>
## related right

*n.* 1. *(plural-normally)* **相關權 /相關權, 鄰接權 /鄰接權** — A right of a creative work not connected with the work's actual author.

<a id="entry-r-001338"></a>
## related to

*adj.* 1. **有關聯+的, 有關係+的** — being connected either logically or causally or by shared characteristics
*“painting and the related arts”*
*“school-related activities”*

<a id="entry-r-001339"></a>
## relatedness

*n.* 1. **關聯性** — a particular manner of connectedness
*“the relatedness of all living things”*

<a id="entry-r-001340"></a>
## relation

**發音：** /ɹɪˈleɪʃ(ə)n/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **關係 /關係** — The manner in which two things may be associated.
2. **家屬, 親屬, 親戚** — a person related by blood or marriage
*“police are searching for relatives of the deceased”*
*“he has distant relations back in New Jersey”*
3. an abstraction belonging to or characteristic of two entities or parts together
4. the act of sexual procreation between a man and a woman; the man's penis is inserted into the woman's vagina and excited until orgasm and ejaculation occur
5. an act of narration
*“he was the hero according to his own relation”*
*“his endless recounting of the incident eventually became unbearable”*
6. (law) the principle that an act done at a later time is deemed by law to have occurred at an earlier time
*“his attorney argued for the relation back of the amended complaint to the time the initial complaint was filed”*

<a id="entry-r-001341"></a>
## relational

*adj.* 1. **有關係+的, 有聯繫+的** — having a relation or being related

<a id="entry-r-001342"></a>
## relational adjective

*n.* 1. **關係形容詞 /關係形容詞** — A classifying adjective; an adjective that defines a relation and not a quality, commonly found in (but not exclusive to) languages that do not permit noun adjuncts.
2. an adjective that classifies its noun (e.g., `a nervous disease' or `a musical instrument')

<a id="entry-r-001343"></a>
## relations

*n.* 1. **關係** — mutual dealings or connections or communications among persons or groups

<a id="entry-r-001344"></a>
## relations of production

*n.* 1. *(Marxism, plural, plural-normally)* **生產關係 /生產關係** — The sum total of social relationships that people must enter into in order to survive, produce, and reproduce their means of life.

<a id="entry-r-001345"></a>
## relationship

**發音：** /ɹɪˈleɪʃ(ə)nˌʃɪp/
*n.* 1. **聯繫, 關係** — a relation between people; (`relationship' is often used where `relation' would serve, as in `the relationship between inflation and unemployment', but the preferred usage of `relationship' is for human relations or states of relatedness)
*“the relationship between mothers and their children”*
2. **關係** — a state of connectedness between people (especially an emotional connection)
*“he didn't want his wife to know of the relationship”*
3. **親屬關係** — a state involving mutual dealings between people or parties or countries
4. **關係 /關係** — A connection or association; the condition of being related.
5. **親屬關係 /親屬關係, 血緣 /血緣, 親屬 /親屬** — Kinship; being related by blood or marriage.
6. **親屬關係, 關係** — (anthropology) relatedness or connection by blood or marriage or adoption

<a id="entry-r-001346"></a>
## relative

**發音：** /ˈɹɛl.ə.tɪv/
*adj.* 1. **相對+的, 相比較+的** — estimated by comparison; not absolute or complete
*“a relative stranger”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **有關的 /有關的, 相對的 /相對的, 關連的 /關連的** — Depending on an antecedent; comparative.
3. properly related in size or degree or other measurable characteristics; usually followed by `to'
*“the punishment ought to be proportional to the crime”*
*“earnings relative to production”*
*n.* 1. **家屬, 親屬, 親戚** — a person related by blood or marriage
*“police are searching for relatives of the deceased”*
*“he has distant relations back in New Jersey”*
2. **親戚 /親戚** — Someone connected by blood, marriage, or adoption; someone in the same family.
3. an animal or plant that bears a relationship to another (as related by common descent or by membership in the same genus)

<a id="entry-r-001347"></a>
## relative adjective

*n.* 1. **關係形容詞 /關係形容詞** — A relative pronoun used in an adjectival clause.

<a id="entry-r-001348"></a>
## relative atomic mass

*n.* 1. **相對原子質量 /相對原子質量** — The ratio of the average mass per atom of the naturally occurring form of an element to 1/12 of the mass of a carbon-12 atom. Symbol Aᵣ; abbreviation RAM or r.a.m. For elements with several isotopes, the most stable isotope is treated as the representative.
2. (chemistry) the mass of an atom of a chemical element expressed in atomic mass units

<a id="entry-r-001349"></a>
## relative clause

*n.* 1. **關係字句 /關係字句, 關係從句 /關係從句, 定語從句 /定語從句** — A subordinate clause that modifies or gives further information about a noun or sometimes a whole statement; in English, it may be introduced by a relative pronoun (sometimes optional) such as that, which or who.
2. a clause introduced by a relative pronoun
*“`who visits frequently' is a relative clause in the sentence `John, who visits frequently, is ill'”*

<a id="entry-r-001350"></a>
## relative frequency

*n.* 1. **頻率** — the ratio of the number of observations in a statistical category to the total number of observations

<a id="entry-r-001351"></a>
## relative humidity

*n.* 1. **相對溼度** — the ratio of the amount of water in the air at a give temperature to the maximum amount it could hold at that temperature; expressed as a percentage
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **相對濕度 /相對溼度** — The ratio of the actual amount of water vapor (absolute humidity) present in the air to the saturation point at the same temperature, usually expressed as a percentage.

<a id="entry-r-001352"></a>
## relative incidence

*n.* 1. **發生頻率** — the relative frequency of occurrence of something

<a id="entry-r-001353"></a>
## relative majority

*n.* 1. **相對多數** — (in an election with more than 2 options) the number of votes for the candidate or party receiving the greatest number (but less that half of the votes)

<a id="entry-r-001354"></a>
## relative pronoun

**發音：** /ˈɹɛl.ə.tɪv ˈpɹəʊ.naʊn/
*n.* 1. **關係代名詞 /關係代名詞** — A pronoun that introduces a relative clause and refers to an antecedent. In English, some words that can be used as interrogative pronouns can alternatively be used as relative pronouns: which, who, whose, whom and (non-standard) what. The other English relative pronouns are whoever, whomever, whatever, and that.
2. a pronoun (as `that' or `which' or `who') that introduces a relative clause referring to some antecedent

<a id="entry-r-001355"></a>
## relative quantity

*n.* 1. **相對值, 相對數量** — a quantity relative to some purpose

<a id="entry-r-001356"></a>
## relatively

**發音：** /ˈɹɛl.ə.tɪv.li/
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **比較 /比較** — In a relative manner; with reference to environment or competition; contextually or comparatively.
2. in a relative manner; by comparison to something else
*“the situation is relatively calm now”*

<a id="entry-r-001357"></a>
## relativism

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **相對主義 /相對主義** — The theory, especially in ethics or aesthetics, that conceptions of truth and moral values are not absolute but are relative to the persons or groups holding them.
2. (philosophy) the philosophical doctrine that all criteria of judgment are relative to the individuals and situations involved

<a id="entry-r-001358"></a>
## relativistic

*adj.* 1. **相對論性+的, 相對論+的** — relating or subject to the special or the general theory of relativity
*“relativistic quantum mechanics”*
*“relativistic increase in mass”*
2. **相對主義+的, 相對論+的** — of or relating to the philosophical doctrine of relativism

<a id="entry-r-001359"></a>
## relativity

**發音：** /ɹɛl.əˈtɪv.ɪ.ti/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **相對性 /相對性, 關聯性 /關聯性, 相關性 /相關性** — The state of being relative to something else; the absence of universally applicable rules or standards; relativism; (countable) an instance of this.
2. (physics) the theory that space and time are relative concepts rather than absolute concepts
3. the quality of being relative and having significance only in relation to something else

<a id="entry-r-001360"></a>
## relativizer

*n.* 1. **関係詞節標識 /関系詞節標識** — A grammatical element used to indicate a relative clause.

<a id="entry-r-001361"></a>
## relax

*v.* 1. **解壓, 減壓, 放鬆** — become less tense, rest, or take one's ease
*“He relaxed in the hot tub”*
*“Let's all relax after a hard day's work”*
2. **使變鬆, 放鬆, 使鬆弛** — make less taut
*“relax the tension on the rope”*
3. **變得不緊, 變得更松, 變鬆** — become loose or looser or less tight
*“The noose loosened”*
*“the rope relaxed”*
4. *(intransitive)* **放鬆下來 /放鬆下來** — To become loose.
5. *(intransitive)* **鬆弛 /鬆弛** — To rest and become relieved of stress.
6. **變從容, 變得輕鬆, 鬆懈, 緩和** — become less tense, less formal, or less restrained, and assume a friendlier manner
*“our new colleague relaxed when he saw that we were a friendly group”*

*adj.* 1. **弛緩+的, 有弛緩作用的, 有緩和力的** — tending to relax or relieve muscular or nervous tension
*“a relaxant drug”*

<a id="entry-r-001363"></a>
## relaxation

**發音：** /ˌriː.lækˈseɪ.ʃən/
*n.* 1. **弛松** — (physiology) the gradual lengthening of inactive muscle or muscle fibers
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **鬆弛 /鬆弛** — The act of relaxing or the state of being relaxed; the opposite of stress or tension; the aim of recreation and leisure activities.
3. **張弛** — (physics) the exponential return of a system to equilibrium after a disturbance
4. **放鬆, 休息** — a feeling of refreshing tranquility and an absence of tension or worry
*“the easiness we feel when sleeping”*
5. **自由主義化, 自由化** — the act of making less strict
6. an occurrence of control or strength weakening
*“the relaxation of requirements”*
*“the loosening of his grip”*

<a id="entry-r-001364"></a>

*“the relay was successful”*
3. a crew of workers who relieve another crew
4. a fresh team to relieve weary draft animals

## release

**發音：** /ɹəˈliːs/
*v.* 1. **使自由, 解放, 豁免, 釋放** — grant freedom to; free from confinement
2. **出版, 發佈, 發表, 發行** — prepare and issue for public distribution or sale
*“publish a magazine or newspaper”*
3. *(transitive)* **釋放 /釋放** — To free or liberate; to set free.
4. **放棄, 讓渡, 交出, 免除, 辭去** — part with a possession or right
*“I am relinquishing my bedroom to the long-term house guest”*
*“resign a claim to the throne”*
5. release, as from one's grip
*“Let go of the door handle, please!”*
*“relinquish your grip on the rope--you won't fall”*
6. let (something) fall or spill from a container
*“turn the flour onto a plate”*
*n.* 1. **放出, 鬆開, 釋放, 解放** — a process that liberates or discharges something
*“there was a sudden release of oxygen”*
*“the release of iodine from the thyroid gland”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **發行 /發行, 發布 /發佈** — The distribution, either public or private, of an initial or new and upgraded version of a computer software product.
3. **清還證明書** — a legal document evidencing the discharge of a debt or obligation
4. **棄權書, 棄權證書, 解僱, 豁免, 免除** — a formal written statement of relinquishment
5. merchandise issued for sale or public showing (especially a record or film)
*“a new release from the London Symphony Orchestra”*
6. the act of liberating someone or something

<a id="entry-r-001374"></a>
## releasing factor

*n.* 1. **釋放因子** — a substance produced by the hypothalamus that is capable of accelerating the secretion of a given hormone by the anterior pituitary gland
2. any of several hormones produced in the hypothalamus and carried by a vein to the anterior pituitary gland where they stimulate the release of anterior pituitary hormones; each of these hormones causes the anterior pituitary to secrete a specific hormone

<a id="entry-r-001375"></a>
## releasing hormone

*n.* 1. **釋放因子** — a substance produced by the hypothalamus that is capable of accelerating the secretion of a given hormone by the anterior pituitary gland
2. any of several hormones produced in the hypothalamus and carried by a vein to the anterior pituitary gland where they stimulate the release of anterior pituitary hormones; each of these hormones causes the anterior pituitary to secrete a specific hormone

## relegate

*v.* 1. **委託** — refer to another person for decision or judgment
*“She likes to relegate difficult questions to her colleagues”*
2. **使降低地位, 使降等, 使降級** — assign to a lower position; reduce in rank
*“She was demoted because she always speaks up”*
*“He was broken down to Sergeant”*
3. **驅逐** — expel, as if by official decree
*“he was banished from his own country”*
4. assign to a class or kind
*“How should algae be classified?”*
*“People argue about how to relegate certain mushrooms”*

*“rectify the inequities in salaries”*
3. provide relief for
*“remedy his illness”*
*n.* 1. **醫藥, 治療法, 治療** — a medicine or therapy that cures disease or relieve pain
2. **補救措施 /補救措施** — Something that corrects or counteracts.
3. **補救方法 /補救方法** — The legal means to recover a right or to prevent or obtain redress for a wrong.
4. **治療 /治療, 療法 /療法** — A medicine, application, or treatment that relieves or cures a disease.
5. act of correcting an error or a fault or an evil

## renounce

**發音：** /ɹɪˈnaʊns/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **放棄 /放棄** — To give up, resign, surrender.
2. **禪讓, 讓出, 退位, 放棄, 退出** — give up, such as power, as of monarchs and emperors, or duties and obligations
*“The King abdicated when he married a divorcee”*
3. **辭去, 辭職, 離開** — leave (a job, post, or position) voluntarily
*“She vacated the position when she got pregnant”*
*“The chairman resigned when he was found to have misappropriated funds”*
4. **堅決放棄, 發誓放棄, 放棄** — turn away from; give up
*“I am foreswearing women forever”*
5. cast off
*“She renounced her husband”*
*“The parents repudiated their son”*

<a id="entry-r-001505"></a>
## renovate

**發音：** /ˈɹɛn.nə(ʊ).veɪt/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **裝修 /裝修, 修復 /修復, 翻新** — To renew; to revamp something to make it look new again.
2. restore to a previous or better condition
*“They renovated the ceiling of the Sistine Chapel”*
3. make brighter and prettier
*“we refurbished the guest wing”*
*“My wife wants us to renovate”*
4. give new life or energy to
*“A hot soup will revive me”*
*“This will renovate my spirits”*

<a id="entry-r-001506"></a>

*“a major overhal of the healthcare system was proposed”*

## rent

**發音：** /ˈɹɛnt/
*n.* 1. **房租, 租價, 租金** — a payment or series of payments made by the lessee to an owner for use of some property, facility, equipment, or service
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **租金, 房租, 租價 /租價** — A payment made by a tenant at intervals in order to lease a property.
3. **經濟地租, 經濟租金** — the return derived from cultivated land in excess of that derived from the poorest land cultivated under similar conditions
4. **拉裂, 撕破, 撕裂, 裂開, 劈開** — the act of rending or ripping or splitting something
*“he gave the envelope a vigorous rip”*
5. an opening made forcibly as by pulling apart
*“there was a rip in his pants”*
*“she had snags in her stockings”*
*v.* 1. **租, 出租, 租出** — let for money
*“We rented our apartment to friends while we were abroad”*
2. **出租, 租出** — grant use or occupation of under a term of contract
*“I am leasing my country estate to some foreigners”*
3. **出租, 租借, 租出, 租賃, 租** — engage for service under a term of contract
*“We took an apartment on a quiet street”*
*“Let's rent a car”*
4. *(transitive)* **租, 租借, 租賃 /租賃** — To take a lease of premises in exchange for rent.
5. *(informal, transitive)* **出租** — To grant a lease in return for rent.
6. *(transitive)* **租借, 租用, 租賃 /租賃** — To obtain or have temporary possession of an object (e.g. a movie) in exchange for money.

## repair

**發音：** /ɹɪˈpɛə/
*v.* 1. **修理, 修補, 修復** — restore by replacing a part or putting together what is torn or broken
*“She repaired her TV set”*
*“Repair my shoes please”*
2. **修理** — To restore to good working order, fix, or improve damaged condition; to mend; to remedy.
3. **賠償, 補償** — make amends for; pay compensation for
*“One can never fully repair the suffering and losses of the Jews in the Third Reich”*
*“She was compensated for the loss of her arm in the accident”*
4. move, travel, or proceed toward some place
*“He repaired to his cabin in the woods”*
5. set straight or right
*“remedy these deficiencies”*
*“rectify the inequities in salaries”*
6. give new life or energy to
*“A hot soup will revive me”*
*“This will renovate my spirits”*
*n.* 1. **狀態** — a formal way of referring to the condition of something
*“the building was in good repair”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **修理** — The act of repairing something.
3. the act of putting something in working order again
4. a frequently visited place

## replace

**發音：** /ɹɪˈpleɪs/
*v.* 1. **更換** — substitute a person or thing for (another that is broken or inefficient or lost or no longer working or yielding what is expected)

2. **接替, 替換, 代替, 取代** — take the place or move into the position of
*“Smith replaced Miller as CEO after Miller left”*
*“the computer has supplanted the slide rule”*

*“the con artist replaced the original with a fake Rembrandt”*
*“substitute regular milk with fat-free milk”*
4. *(transitive)* **取代, 撤換 /撤換, 交替, 替代, 更換 /更換, 代替** — To supply or substitute an equivalent with.

*“replace the book on the shelf after you have finished reading it”*
*“please put the clean dishes back in the cabinet when you have washed them”*

*“he got an A on his composition”*

2. **爬蟲 /爬蟲, 爬行類 /爬行類, 爬蟲類 /爬蟲類** — A (typically scaly and egg-laying) cold-blooded vertebrate tetrapod animal, of the class Reptilia (excluding birds), such as a lizard, turtle, snake, crocodile, alligator, etc.

## Republican

**發音：** /ɹɨˈpʌ.blɨ.kɨn/
*n.* 1. *(US)* **共和黨人 /共和黨人** — A member or supporter of the Republican Party, the more right-wing of the two main political parties in the United States.

<a id="entry-r-001683"></a>
## republican

**發音：** /ɹɪˈpʌblɪkən/
*n.* 1. **共和主義者 /共和主義者** — Someone who favors a republic as a form of government.
2. a member of the Republican Party
3. an advocate of a republic (usually in opposition to a monarchy)
4. a tributary of the Kansas River that flows from eastern Colorado eastward through Nebraska and Kansas
*adj.* 1. **共和黨+的** — relating to or belonging to the Republican Party
*“a Republican senator”*
*“Republican party politics”*
2. **支持共和制的** — Advocating or supporting a republic as a form of government, advocating or supporting republicanism.
3. **共和國的 /共和國的** — Of or belonging to a republic.
4. having the supreme power lying in the body of citizens entitled to vote for officers and representatives responsible to them or characteristic of such government
*“the United States shall guarantee to every state in this union a republican form of government"- United States Constitution”*
*“a very republican notion”*

<a id="entry-r-001684"></a>
## republicanism

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **共和, 共和主義 /共和主義** — The political ideology of being a citizen in a state as a republic under which the people hold popular sovereignty.
2. the political orientation of those who hold that a republic is the best form of government

<a id="entry-r-001685"></a>

**發音：** /ɹɪˈpʊblɪkə ˈsɜː(ɹ)pskə/

<a id="entry-r-001686"></a>
## repudiation

**發音：** /ɹɪˌpjuːdɪˈeɪ̯ʃ(ə)n/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **拒絕 /拒絕** — The act of refusing to accept; the act of repudiating.
2. rejecting or disowning or disclaiming as invalid
*“Congressional repudiation of the treaty that the President had negotiated”*
3. refusal to acknowledge or pay a debt or honor a contract (especially by public authorities)
*“the repudiation of the debt by the city”*
4. the exposure of falseness or pretensions
*“the debunking of religion has been too successful”*

<a id="entry-r-001687"></a>
## repugnance

**發音：** /ɹɪˈpʌɡnəns/
*n.* 1. **反感** — intense aversion
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **厭惡** — Contradiction, inconsistency, incompatibility, incongruity; an instance of such.
3. **不一致, 矛盾** — the relation between propositions that cannot both be true at the same time

<a id="entry-r-001688"></a>
## repugnant

*adj.* 1. **可惡+的, 厭惡+的** — offensive to the mind
*“an abhorrent deed”*
*“the obscene massacre at Wounded Knee”*

<a id="entry-r-001689"></a>
## repulsion

*n.* 1. **反感** — intense aversion
2. the force by which bodies repel one another
3. the act of repulsing or repelling an attack; a successful defensive stand

<a id="entry-r-001690"></a>
## repulsive

**發音：** /ɹɪˈpʌlsɪv/
*adj.* 1. **可惡+的, 厭惡+的** — offensive to the mind
*“an abhorrent deed”*
*“the obscene massacre at Wounded Knee”*
2. **令人厭惡的** — Tending to rouse aversion or to repulse; disgusting.
3. possessing the ability to repel
*“a repulsive force”*
4. so extremely ugly as to be terrifying
*“a hideous scar”*
*“a repulsive mask”*

<a id="entry-r-001691"></a>
## repurchase

*n.* 1. the act of purchasing back something previously sold
*v.* 1. **買回, 再買, 再購買, 再採購, 購回** — buy what had previously been sold, lost, or given away
*“He bought back the house that his father sold years ago”*

<a id="entry-r-001692"></a>
## reputable

**發音：** /ˈɹɛpjʊtəbəl/
*adj.* 1. **享有信譽+的, 名聲好+的, 有聲望+的** — having a good reputation
*“a reputable business”*
*“a reputable scientist”*
2. **光彩, 有聲望的 /有聲望的, 有口皆碑的** — Having a good reputation; honourable.

<a id="entry-r-001693"></a>
## reputation

**發音：** /ˌɹɛpjʊˈteɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **名氣, 聲譽, 名聲, 聲望, 好名聲** — the state of being held in high esteem and honor
2. **壞的聲譽** — notoriety for some particular characteristic
*“his reputation for promiscuity”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **名譽 /名譽, 聲譽 /聲譽, 名聲 /名聲, 聲望 /聲望** — What somebody or something is known for.
4. the general estimation that the public has for a person
*“he acquired a reputation as an actor before he started writing”*
*“he was a person of bad report”*

<a id="entry-r-001694"></a>
## repute

**發音：** /ɹɪˈpjuːt/
*v.* 1. **對聲望 /對聲望** — To attribute or credit something to something; to impute.
2. look on as or consider
*“she looked on this affair as a joke”*
*“He thinks of himself as a brilliant musician”*
*n.* 1. **名氣, 聲譽, 名聲, 聲望, 好名聲** — the state of being held in high esteem and honor
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **聲望 /聲望** — Reputation, especially a good reputation.

<a id="entry-r-001695"></a>
## reputedly

*adv.* 1. **一般認為, 據說, 根據風評** — by repute; according to general belief
*“fish with reputedly poisonous flesh”*

<a id="entry-r-001696"></a>
## requel

*n.* 1. **新傳 /新傳, 別傳 /別傳, 外傳 /外傳** — A movie which revisits the subject matter of an earlier film but is not a remake or a linear continuation of its plot (i.e. a sequel or prequel).

<a id="entry-r-001697"></a>
## request

**發音：** /ɹɪˈkwɛst/
*v.* 1. **拜託, 請求, 要求** — express the need or desire for; ask for
*“She requested an extra bed in her room”*
*“She called for room service”*
2. **請求 /請求, 拜託, 要求** — To ask for (something).
3. ask (a person) to do something
*“She asked him to be here at noon”*
*“I requested that she type the entire manuscript”*
4. inquire for (information)
*“I requested information from the secretary”*
*n.* 1. **請願, 請願書, 請求, 要求** — a formal message requesting something that is submitted to an authority
2. **請求 /請求, 要求** — Act of requesting (with the adposition at in the presence of possessives, and on in their absence).
3. **請求 /請求** — A message sent over a network to a server.
4. the verbal act of requesting

<a id="entry-r-001698"></a>
## request stop

*n.* 1. **招呼站** — A location at which public transport does not ordinarily stop, but will do so if specifically requested by a passenger.

<a id="entry-r-001699"></a>
## requested

*adj.* 1. **被要求的, 被請求的** — asked for
*“the requested aid is forthcoming”*

<a id="entry-r-001700"></a>
## requiem

**發音：** /ˈɹɛ.kwi.əm/
*n.* 1. **安魂曲** — A musical composition for such a mass.
2. a song or hymn of mourning composed or performed as a memorial to a dead person
3. a musical setting for a Mass celebrating the dead
4. a Mass celebrated for the dead

<a id="entry-r-001701"></a>
## require

**發音：** /ɹɪˈkwaɪə/
*v.* 1. **要求, 必要, 要, 需, 需求** — require as useful, just, or proper
*“It takes nerve to do what she did”*
*“success usually requires hard work”*
2. **期望, 要求** — consider obligatory; request and expect
*“We require our secretary to be on time”*
*“Aren't we asking too much of these children?”*
3. **指令, 命令** — make someone do something
4. **必要, 必須, 需, 要, 需要** — have need of
*“This piano wants the attention of a competent tuner”*
5. **要求** — To demand, to insist upon (having); to call for authoritatively.
6. **需要** — Naturally to demand (something) as indispensable; to need, to call for as necessary.

<a id="entry-r-001702"></a>
## requirement

**發音：** /ɹɪˈkwʌɪəm(ə)nt/
*n.* 1. **必需品, 要素, 需要** — anything indispensable
*“food and shelter are necessities of life”*
*“the essentials of the good life”*
2. **要求, 需求** — A necessity or prerequisite; something required or obligatory. Its adpositions are generally of in relation to who or what has given it, on in relation to whom or what it is given to, and for in relation to what is required.
3. required activity
*“the requirements of his work affected his health”*
*“there were many demands on his time”*
4. something that is required in advance
*“Latin was a prerequisite for admission”*

<a id="entry-r-001703"></a>
## requisite

*n.* 1. **必需品, 要素, 需要** — anything indispensable
*“food and shelter are necessities of life”*
*“the essentials of the good life”*
*adj.* 1. necessary for relief or supply
*“provided them with all things needful”*

<a id="entry-r-001704"></a>
## requisiteness

*n.* 1. **必須的** — the state of being absolutely required

<a id="entry-r-001705"></a>
## requisition

*v.* 1. **徵用** — demand and take for use or service, especially by military or public authority for public service
2. make a formal request for official services
*n.* 1. the act of requiring; an authoritative request or demand, especially by a military or public authority that takes something over (usually temporarily) for military or public use
2. an official form on which a request in made
*“first you have to fill out the requisition”*
3. seizing property that belongs to someone else and holding it until profits pay the demand for which it was seized

<a id="entry-r-001706"></a>
## requital

*n.* 1. **懲罰** — a justly deserved penalty
2. an act of requiting; returning in kind

<a id="entry-r-001707"></a>
## requite

*v.* 1. **歸還, 償還** — make repayment for or return something

<a id="entry-r-001708"></a>
## reread

**發音：** /ɹiːˈɹiːd/

## resile

*“The landlord cannot resile from the lease”*
2. **公開認錯, 發誓拋棄, 發誓放棄, 宣佈放棄, 放棄信仰** — formally reject or disavow a formerly held belief, usually under pressure
*“He retracted his earlier statements about his religion”*
*“She abjured her beliefs”*
3. **彈回** — return to the original position or state after being stretched or compressed
*“The rubber tubes resile”*
4. spring back; spring away from an impact
*“The rubber ball bounced”*

5. a statement that solves a problem or explains how to solve the problem
*“they were trying to find a peaceful solution”*
*“the answers were in the back of the book”*
6. the semantic role of the noun phrase whose referent exists only by virtue of the activity denoted by the verb in the clause
*v.* 1. **後果, 結果是, 導致** — issue or terminate (in a specified way, state, etc.); end
*“result in tragedy”*
2. **導致, 致使, 留** — have as a result or residue
*“The water left a mark on the silk dress”*
*“Her blood left a stain on the napkin”*
3. come about or follow as a consequence
*“nothing will result from this meeting”*

<a id="entry-r-001897"></a>
## resultant

*adj.* 1. following or accompanying as a consequence
*“an excessive growth of bureaucracy, with attendant problems”*
*“snags incidental to the changeover in management”*
*n.* 1. **結果** — the final point in a process
2. **結果** — something that results
*“he listened for the results on the radio”*
3. a vector that is the sum of two or more other vectors

<a id="entry-r-001898"></a>

*“We resumed the negotiations”*

*“resume a title”*

3. *(transitive)* **恢復 /恢復, 重新開始 /重新開始** — To start (something) again that has been stopped or paused from the point at which it was stopped or paused; continue, carry on.

*“The painting resumed its old condition when we restored it”*
5. give a summary (of)

*“He backed out of his earlier promise”*

*“the disorderly retreat of French troops”*
4. a place of privacy; a place affording peace and quiet
5. (military) a signal to begin a withdrawal from a dangerous position
6. (military) a bugle call signaling the lowering of the flag at sunset

## revolution

**發音：** /ˌɹɛv.əˈluː.ʃən/
*n.* 1. **變革, 革命** — the overthrow of a government by those who are governed
2. *(countable)* **革命, 改革** — A political upheaval in a government or state characterized by great change.
3. *(countable)* **革命** — The popular removal and replacement of a government, especially by sudden violent action.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **革命** — A sudden, vast change in a situation, a discipline, or the way of thinking and behaving.
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **旋轉 /旋轉** — Rotation: the turning of an object around an axis, one complete turn of an object during rotation.
6. **公轉, 轉動, 旋轉, 運轉** — a single complete turn (axial or orbital)
*“the plane made three rotations before it crashed”*
*“the revolution of the earth about the sun takes one year”*

<a id="entry-r-002107"></a>

2. **革命性的** — Of or pertaining to something that portends of great change; overthrowing a standing mindset
3. **引起旋轉+的, 能旋轉+的, 能轉動+的, 轉動+的** — of or relating to or characteristic or causing an axial or orbital turn

*“revolutionary wars”*
*“the Revolutionary era”*

*“revolutionary pamphlets”*
*“a revolutionary junta”*
6. markedly new or introducing radical change
*“a revolutionary discovery”*
*“radical political views”*

2. a radical supporter of political or social revolution

*n.* 1. **獼猴 /獼猴** — The rhesus macaque, Macaca mulatta
2. **恆河猴, 獼猴** — of southern Asia; used in medical research

<a id="entry-r-002179"></a>
## rhetoric

**發音：** /ˈɹɛtəɹɪk/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **修辭 /修辭, 修辭學 /修辭學** — The art of using language, especially public speaking, as a means to persuade.
2. *(countable, derogatory, sometimes)* **強詞奪理 /強詞奪理** — Meaningless language with an exaggerated style intended to impress.
3. **修辭, 修辭法** — study of the technique and rules for using language effectively (especially in public speaking)
4. using language effectively to please or persuade
5. high-flown style; excessive use of verbal ornamentation
*“the grandiosity of his prose”*
*“an excessive ornateness of language”*
6. loud and confused and empty talk
*“mere rhetoric”*

<a id="entry-r-002180"></a>
## rhetorical

*adj.* 1. **修辭學+的** — of or relating to rhetoric
*“accepted two or three verbal and rhetorical changes I suggested"- W.A.White”*
*“the rhetorical sin of the meaningless variation"- Lewis Mumford”*
2. **誇張+的, 浮誇+的** — given to rhetoric, emphasizing style at the expense of thought
*“mere rhetorical frippery”*

<a id="entry-r-002181"></a>
## rhetorical device

*n.* 1. **修辭手法 /修辭手法, 修辭手段 /修辭手段** — A phrase (set phrase or created phrase) that uses reduplication, onomatopoeia, or other phonetic markers that increase memorability and “musicality” (making rhetoric pleasing or entertaining).
2. a use of language that creates a literary effect (but often without regard for literal significance)

<a id="entry-r-002182"></a>
## rhetorical question

*n.* 1. **反問句 /反問句** — A question posed only for dramatic or persuasive effect.
2. a statement that is formulated as a question but that is not supposed to be answered
*“he liked to make his points with rhetorical questions”*

<a id="entry-r-002183"></a>

## rheumatic

*n.* 1. a person suffering with rheumatism
*adj.* 1. **關節炎+的, 患風溼症+的, 風溼症+的** — of or pertaining to arthritis
*“my creaky old joints”*
*“rheumy with age and grief”*

<a id="entry-r-002191"></a>
## rheumatic fever

*n.* 1. **迴歸熱** — a severe disease chiefly of children and characterized by painful inflammation of the joints and frequently damage to the heart valves
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **風濕熱 /風溼熱, 風濕性熱 /風溼性熱** — An inflammation of the joints and the heart following a respiratory infection with streptococcus.

<a id="entry-r-002192"></a>
## rheumatic heart disease

*n.* 1. **風溼性心臟病** — heart disease caused by recurrent episodes of rheumatic fever; characterized by changes in the myocardium or scarring of the heart valves that reduce the power of the heart to pump blood

<a id="entry-r-002193"></a>
## rheumatism

**發音：** /ˈɹu.məˌtɪz.əm/
*n.* 1. **風溼, 風溼病, 風溼症** — any painful disorder of the joints or muscles or connective tissues
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **風濕 /風溼, 風濕病 /風溼病** — Any disorder of the muscles, tendons, joints, bones, nerves, characterized by pain, discomfort and disability.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **類風濕性關節炎 /類風溼性關節炎** — Atrophic arthritis, rheumatoid arthritis
4. a chronic autoimmune disease with inflammation of the joints and marked deformities; something (possibly a virus) triggers an attack on the synovium by the immune system, which releases cytokines that stimulate an inflammatory reaction that can lead to the destruction of all components of the joint

<a id="entry-r-002194"></a>
## rheumatoid

*adj.* 1. **關節炎+的, 患風溼症+的, 風溼症+的** — of or pertaining to arthritis
*“my creaky old joints”*
*“rheumy with age and grief”*
2. *(dated, not-comparable, usually)* **類風濕病的 /類風溼病的, 類風溼病的** — Presenting analogies with rheumatism.

<a id="entry-r-002195"></a>
## rheumatoid arthritis

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **類風濕性關節炎 /類風溼性關節炎** — A chronic and progressive disease in which the immune system attacks the joints. It is characterised by pain, inflammation and swelling of the joints, stiffness, weakness, loss of mobility and deformity. Tissues throughout the body can be affected, including the skin, blood vessels, heart, lungs, and muscles.
2. a chronic autoimmune disease with inflammation of the joints and marked deformities; something (possibly a virus) triggers an attack on the synovium by the immune system, which releases cytokines that stimulate an inflammatory reaction that can lead to the destruction of all components of the joint

<a id="entry-r-002196"></a>
## rheumatoid factor

*n.* 1. **類風溼因子** — autoantibody that is usually present in the serum of people with rheumatoid arthritis

<a id="entry-r-002197"></a>
## rheumatology

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **風濕病學 /風溼病學** — The branch of medicine specializing in arthritis and other ailments of the joints.
2. the branch of medicine dealing with the study and treatment of pathologies of the muscles or tendons or joints

**發音：** /ɹaɪm/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **押韻 /押韻** — Rhyming: sameness of letters or sounds of part of some words.

2. **壓韻, 押韻** — be similar in sound, especially with respect to the last syllable

## rhythm

**發音：** /ˈɹɪð.əm/
*n.* 1. **節奏, 旋律** — the basic rhythmic unit in a piece of music
*“the piece has a fast rhythm”*
*“the conductor set the beat”*
2. **循環地** — an interval during which a recurring sequence of events occurs
*“the never-ending cycle of the seasons”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **節奏 /節奏** — The variation of strong and weak elements (such as duration, accent) of sounds, notably in speech or music, over time; a beat or meter.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **節奏 /節奏** — The tempo or speed of a beat, song or repetitive event.
5. recurring at regular intervals
6. the arrangement of spoken words alternating stressed and unstressed elements
*“the rhythm of Frost's poetry”*

<a id="entry-r-002285"></a>

## ribonucleic acid

*“ribonucleic acid is the genetic material of some viruses”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **核糖核酸** — A derivative of DNA having ribose in place of deoxyribose, and uracil in place of thymine; its primary function is in the transcription of genetic material and subsequent synthesis of protein.

<a id="entry-r-002325"></a>
## ribosome

**發音：** /ˈɹaɪbəˌsəʊm/
*n.* 1. **核糖體 /核糖體, 核糖核蛋白體 /核糖核蛋白體, 核蛋白體 /核蛋白體** — A small organelle found in all cells; involved in the production of proteins by translating messenger RNA.
2. an organelle in the cytoplasm of a living cell; they attach to mRNA and move down it one codon at a time and then stop until tRNA brings the required amino acid; when it reaches a stop codon it falls apart and releases the completed protein molecule for use by the cell
*“the ribosome is the site of protein synthesis”*

## rich

*adj.* 1. **闊, 有錢+的, 富+的, 富有+的** — possessing material wealth
*“her father is extremely rich”*
*“many fond hopes are pinned on rich uncles”*
2. **豐產+的, 豐富+的, 富饒+的, 肥沃+的** — having an abundant supply of desirable qualities or substances (especially natural resources)
*“blessed with a land rich in minerals”*

*“a rich collection of antiques”*
4. **極度+的, 濃厚+的, 深度+的, 非常+的, 強烈+的** — strong; intense
*“deep purple”*
*“a rich red”*

6. **富饒+的, 充足+的, 豐富+的** — high in mineral content; having a high proportion of fuel to air
*“a rich vein of copper", "a rich gas mixture”*
*n.* 1. people who have possessions and wealth (considered as a group)

3. the property of a sensation that is rich and pleasing
*“the music had a fullness that echoed through the hall”*
*“the cheap wine had no body, no mellowness”*
4. the quality of having high intrinsic value
*“the richness of the mines and pastureland”*
*“the cut of her clothes and the richness of the fabric were distinctive”*
5. the property of producing abundantly and sustaining vigorous and luxuriant growth
*“he praised the richness of the soil”*
*“weeds lovely in their rankness”*
6. a strong deep vividness of hue
*“the fire-light gave a richness of coloring to that side of the room”*

## Richter scale

**發音：** /ˈɹɪkt.ɚ skeɪl/

*“mineral rights”*
*“film rights”*
6. an abstract idea of that which is due to a person or governmental body by law or tradition or nature
*“they are endowed by their Creator with certain unalienable Rights”*
*“Certain rights can never be granted to the government but must be kept in the hands of the people"- Eleanor Roosevelt”*
*adj.* 1. **右, 右邊+的** — being or located on or directed toward the side of the body to the east when facing north
*“my right hand”*
*“right center field”*
2. **對, 正當+的, 正確+的** — free from error; especially conforming to fact or truth
*“the correct answer”*
*“the correct version”*
3. **正直+的, 正當+的, 正確+的** — in conformance with justice or law or morality
*“do the right thing and confess”*
4. **右傾+的, 保守+的** — of or belonging to the political or intellectual right
5. **供右手用+的, 右手+的** — intended for the right hand
*“a right-hand glove”*
6. socially right or correct
*“it isn't right to leave the party without saying goodbye”*
*“correct behavior”*
*adv.* 1. **右派地, 右邊地, 向右, 往右** — toward or on the right; also used figuratively
*“he looked right and left”*
*“the party has moved right”*
2. precisely, exactly
*“stand right here!”*
3. immediately
*“she called right after dinner”*
4. exactly
*“he fell flop on his face”*
5. in the right manner
*“please do your job properly!”*
*“can't you carry me decent?”*
6. an interjection expressing agreement
*v.* 1. **糾正, 賠償, 補救, 補償** — make reparations or amends for
*“right a wrongs done to the victims of the Holocaust”*
2. **使直, 扶正** — put in or restore to an upright position
*“They righted the sailboat that had capsized”*
3. **恢復平穩** — regain an upright or proper position
*“The capsized boat righted again”*
4. make right or correct
*“Correct the mistakes”*
*“rectify the calculation”*

2. (astronomy) the equatorial coordinate specifying the angle, measured eastward along the celestial equator, from the vernal equinox to the intersection of the hour circle that passes through an object in the sky; usually expressed in hours and minutes and seconds; used with declination to specify positions on the celestial sphere
*“one hour of right ascension equals fifteen degrees”*

## risk

**發音：** /ɹɪsk/
*v.* 1. **不惜, 冒險幹, 冒險** — expose to a chance of loss or damage
*“We risked losing a lot of money in this venture”*
*“Why risk your life?”*
2. **冒險, 賭博, 孤注一擲** — take a risk in the hope of a favorable outcome
*“When you buy these stocks you are gambling”*
*n.* 1. **危險之源, 冒險, 風險, 危險** — a source of danger; a possibility of incurring loss or misfortune
*“drinking alcohol is a health hazard”*
2. **冒險, 危險** — a venture undertaken without regard to possible loss or injury
*“he saw the rewards but not the risks of crime”*
*“there was a danger he would do the wrong thing”*
3. *(uncountable)* **風險 /風險** — The potential negative effect of an event, determined by multiplying the likelihood of the event occurring with its magnitude should it occur.
4. the probability of becoming infected given that exposure to an infectious agent has occurred
5. the probability of being exposed to an infectious agent

<a id="entry-r-002573"></a>
