<a id="entry-r-002574"></a>
## risk assessment

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **風險評估 /風險評估** — The overall process of identifying all the risks to and from an activity and assessing the potential impact of each risk.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **風險評估 /風險評估** — The determination of the potential impact of an individual risk by measuring or otherwise assessing both the likelihood that it will occur and the impact if it should occur, and then combining the result according to an agreed rule to give a single measure of potential impact.

<a id="entry-r-002575"></a>
## risk capital

<a id="entry-r-002576"></a>
## risk factor

<a id="entry-r-002577"></a>
## risk management

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **風險管理 /風險管理** — The process of determining the maximum acceptable level of overall risk to and from a proposed activity, then using risk assessment techniques to determine the initial level of risk and, if this is excessive, developing a strategy to ameliorate appropriate individual risks until the overall level of risk is reduced to an acceptable level.

*n.* 1. **典禮, 禮, 祭典, 儀式** — any customary observance or practice

## rock

*n.* 1. **岩石, 石, 石頭** — a lump or mass of hard consolidated mineral matter
*“he threw a rock at me”*
2. **石頭, 聚合體** — material consisting of the aggregate of minerals like those making up the Earth's crust
*“that mountain is solid rock”*
*“stone is abundant in New England and there are many quarries”*
3. **搖滾, 搖滾樂, 搖滾音樂** — a genre of popular music originating in the 1950s; a blend of black rhythm-and-blues with white country-and-western
*“rock is a generic term for the range of styles that evolved out of rock'n'roll.”*
4. **傾側, 傾斜** — pitching dangerously to one side
5. United States gynecologist and devout Catholic who conducted the first clinical trials of the oral contraceptive pill (1890-1984)
6. (figurative) someone who is strong and stable and dependable
*“he was her rock during the crisis”*
*“Thou art Peter, and upon this rock I will build my church"--Gospel According to Matthew”*
*v.* 1. **搖動, 搖擺, 搖晃** — move back and forth or sideways
*“the ship was rocking”*
*“the tall building swayed”*
2. **使搖動, 使搖晃, 搖動, 搖晃, 搖擺** — cause to move back and forth
*“rock the cradle”*
*“rock the baby”*

## romantic

**發音：** /ɹə(ʊ)ˈmæntɪk/
*adj.* 1. **浪漫+的, 浪漫主義+的** — belonging to or characteristic of Romanticism or the Romantic Movement in the arts
*“romantic poetry”*
2. **戀愛+的, 羅曼蒂克+的, 色情+的** — expressive of or exciting sexual love or romance
*“her amatory affairs”*
*“amorous glances”*
3. *(historical)* **浪漫** — Of a work of literature, a writer etc.: being like or having the characteristics of a romance, or poetic tale of a mythic or quasi-historical time; fantastic.
4. **浪漫** — Having the qualities of romance (in the sense of something appealing deeply to the imagination); invoking on a powerfully sentimental idea of life; evocative, atmospheric.
5. not sensible about practical matters; idealistic and unrealistic
*“as quixotic as a restoration of medieval knighthood”*
*“a romantic disregard for money”*
*n.* 1. **浪漫的人** — A person with romantic character (a character like those of the knights in a mythic romance).
2. **浪漫的人** — A person who is behaving romantically (in a manner befitting someone who feels an idealized form of love).
3. a soulful or amorous idealist
4. an artist of the Romantic Movement or someone influenced by Romanticism

## root

*n.* 1. **根** — (botany) the usually underground organ that lacks buds or leaves or nodes; absorbs water and mineral salts; usually it anchors the plant to the ground
2. **根源, 起因, 開始, 起源, 開端** — the place where something begins, where it springs into being
*“the Italian beginning of the Renaissance”*
*“Jupiter was the origin of the radiation”*
3. **根** — a number that, when multiplied by itself some number of times, equals a given number
4. **祖先, 祖宗** — someone from whom you are descended (but usually more remote than a grandparent)
5. (linguistics) the form of a word after all affixes are removed
*“thematic vowels are part of the stem”*
6. the set of values that give a true statement when substituted into an equation
*v.* 1. **根源在於** — come into existence, originate
*“The problem roots in her depression”*
2. **定居** — become settled or established and stable in one's residence or life style
*“He finally settled down”*
3. **使紮根, 使生根** — cause to take roots
4. take root and begin to grow
*“this plant roots quickly”*
5. plant by the roots
6. dig with the snout
*“the pig was rooting for truffles”*

## rotate

**發音：** /ɹəʊˈteɪt/
*v.* 1. **公轉, 繞轉, 轉動, 運轉, 旋轉** — turn on or around an axis or a center
*“The Earth revolves around the Sun”*
*“The lamb roast rotates on a spit over the fire”*
2. **使輪流, 輪換, 輪流** — exchange on a regular basis
*“We rotate the lead soprano every night”*
3. *(intransitive)* **旋轉, 轉動** — To spin, turn, or revolve.
4. *(intransitive)* **輪換, 輪流** — To advance through a sequence; to take turns.
5. **使轉動, 使旋轉, 旋轉, 轉動** — cause to turn on an axis or center
*“Rotate the handle”*
6. **向外彎曲, 外傾, 轉出, 展開** — turn outward
*“These birds can splay out their toes”*
*“ballet dancers can rotate their legs out by 90 degrees”*

<a id="entry-r-003060"></a>

<a id="entry-r-003061"></a>
## rotation

**發音：** /ɹoʊˈteɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **轉動, 旋轉** — the act of rotating as if on an axis
*“the rotation of the dancer kept time with the music”*
2. *(uncountable)* **迴轉 /迴轉, 旋轉 /旋轉, 自轉 /自轉** — The act of turning around a centre or an axis.
3. **旋轉** — (mathematics) a transformation in which the coordinate axes are rotated by a fixed angle about the origin
4. **公轉, 轉動, 旋轉, 運轉** — a single complete turn (axial or orbital)
*“the plane made three rotations before it crashed”*
*“the revolution of the earth about the sun takes one year”*
5. a planned recurrent sequence (of crops or personnel etc.)
*“crop rotation makes a balanced demand on the fertility of the soil”*
*“the manager had only four starting pitchers in his rotation”*

## round

**發音：** /ˈɹaʊ̯nd/
*adj.* 1. **圓+的, 環形+的** — having a circular shape
2. *(physical)* **圓 /圓** — Circular or cylindrical; having a circular cross-section in one direction.
3. *(physical)* **圓 /圓** — Lacking sharp angles; having gentle curves.
4. **圓 /圓)** — Complete, whole, not lacking.
5. **整數, 圓唇 /圓唇** — Pronounced with the lips drawn together; rounded.
6. (of sounds) full and rich
*“orotund tones”*
*“the rotund and reverberating phrase”*
*v.* 1. *(intransitive, transitive)* **取整, 四捨五入 /四捨五入** — To approximate (a number, especially a decimal number) by the closest whole number, or some other close number, especially a whole number of hundreds, thousands, etc.; see also round down, round up.
2. **抨擊, 責罵, 攻擊** — attack in speech or writing
*“The editors of the left-leaning paper attacked the new House Speaker”*
3. wind around; move along a circular course
*“round the bend”*
4. make round
*“round the edges”*
5. pronounce with rounded lips
6. bring to a highly developed, finished, or refined state
*“polish your social manners”*
*adv.* 1. from beginning to end; throughout
*“It rains all year round on Skye”*
*“frigid weather the year around”*
*n.* 1. **循環地** — an interval during which a recurring sequence of events occurs
*“the never-ending cycle of the seasons”*
2. **日常事務** — the usual activities in your day
*“the doctor made his rounds”*
3. **回合** — (sports) a division during which one team is on the offensive
4. **回合** — One of the specified pre-determined segments of the total time of a sport event, such as a boxing or wrestling match, during which contestants compete before being signaled to stop.
5. **回合** — A stage in a competition.
6. **圓 /圓** — A circular or spherical object or part of an object.

*“my sweater scratches”*

**發音：** /ˈɹʌdəː/
*n.* 1. **舵, 船舵** — An underwater vane used to steer a vessel. The rudder is controlled by means of a wheel, tiller or other apparatus (modern vessels can be controlled even with a joystick or an autopilot).
2. **舵, 方向舵** — A control surface on the vertical stabilizer of a fixed-wing aircraft or an autogyro. On some craft, the entire vertical stabilizer comprises the rudder. The rudder is controlled by foot-operated control pedals.

## ruin

**發音：** /ˈɹuː.ɪn/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **破壞 /破壞, 毀 /毀** — To cause the fiscal ruin of; to bankrupt or drive out of business.
2. **使破產, 毀, 葬送, 使毀滅, 毀壞** — destroy or cause to fail
*“This behavior will ruin your chances of winning the election”*
3. **破壞 /破壞** — To destroy (e.g. a city) so as to leave ruins.
4. **使毀滅, 毀壞, 推翻, 毀滅, 破壞** — reduce to bankruptcy
*“My daughter's fancy wedding is going to break me!”*
*“The slump in the financial markets smashed him”*
5. destroy completely; damage irreparably
*“You have ruined my car by pouring sugar in the tank!”*
*“The tears ruined her make-up”*
6. reduce to ruins
*“The country lay ruined after the war”*
*n.* 1. **衰敗, 毀滅** — an irrecoverable state of devastation and destruction
*“you have brought ruin on this entire family”*
2. **廢墟, 遺址, 毀滅** — a ruined building
*“they explored several Roman ruins”*
3. *(countable, in-plural, sometimes)* **遺址 /遺址, 廢墟 /廢墟** — The remains of a destroyed or dilapidated construction, such as a house or castle.
4. *(uncountable)* **毀壞 /毀壞, 毀滅 /毀滅** — The state of being a ruin, destroyed or decayed.
5. *(uncountable)* **禍因 /禍因** — Something that leads to serious trouble or destruction.
6. **崩落** — the process of becoming dilapidated

## rule

**發音：** /ɹuːl/
*n.* 1. **準則, 規則, 慣例** — a principle or condition that customarily governs behavior
*“it was his rule to take a walk before breakfast”*
*“short haircuts were the regulation”*
2. **習俗, 標準, 準則, 方案, 慣例** — something regarded as a normative example
*“the convention of not naming the main character”*
*“violence is the rule not the exception”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **規則 /規則, 規律 /規律** — A regulation, law, guideline.
4. *(uncountable)* **統治 /統治** — The act of ruling; administration of law; government; empire; authority; control.
5. **管轄期間, 統治時期, 統治期** — the duration of a monarch's or government's power
*“during the rule of Elizabeth”*
6. **主權, 支配, 支配權, 統治** — dominance or power through legal authority
*“France held undisputed dominion over vast areas of Africa”*
*“the rule of Caesar”*
*v.* 1. **治, 統治, 支配, 管理, 控制** — exercise authority over; as of nations
*“Who is governing the country now?”*
2. **佔上風, 佔優勢, 支配, 統治, 控制** — be larger in number, quantity, power, status or importance
*“Money reigns supreme here”*
*“Hispanics predominate in this neighborhood”*
3. *(stative, transitive)* **治, 統治 /統治, 治理** — To regulate, be in charge of, make decisions for, reign over.
4. decide with authority
*“The King decreed that all firstborn males should be killed”*
5. decide on and make a declaration about
*“find someone guilty”*
6. have an affinity with; of signs of the zodiac

## run

**發音：** /ɹʌn/
*v.* 1. **奔跑, 跑, 跑步** — move fast by using one's feet, with one foot off the ground at any given time
*“Don't run--you'll be out of breath”*
*“The children ran to the store”*
2. **奔, 潛逃, 跑, 逃亡, 逃脫** — flee; take to one's heels; cut and run
*“If you see this man, run!”*
*“The burglars escaped before the police showed up”*
3. **延伸, 達到** — stretch out over a distance, space, time, or scope; run or extend between two points or beyond a certain point
*“Service runs all the way to Cranbury”*
*“His knowledge doesn't go very far”*
4. **操作, 管理** — direct or control; projects, businesses, etc.
*“She is running a relief operation in the Sudan”*
5. **寫出, 寫著, 表現, 說出, 說著** — have a particular form
*“the story or argument runs as follows”*
*“as the saying goes...”*
6. **流, 流動, 流向, 流進** — move along, of liquids
*“Water flowed into the cave”*
*“the Missouri feeds into the Mississippi”*
*n.* 1. **檢驗, 測試, 試驗, 化驗** — the act of testing something
*“in the experimental trials the amount of carbon was measured separately”*
*“he called each flip of the coin a new trial”*
2. **經營時期** — the continuous period of time during which something (a machine or a factory) operates or continues in operation
*“the assembly line was on a 12-hour run”*
3. **產量** — the production achieved during a continuous period of operation (of a machine or factory etc.)
*“a daily run of 100,000 gallons of paint”*
4. **競選運動** — a race between candidates for elective office
*“I managed his campaign for governor”*
*“he is raising money for a Senate run”*
5. a score in baseball made by a runner touching all four bases safely
*“the Yankees scored 3 runs in the bottom of the 9th”*
*“their first tally came in the 3rd inning”*
6. a race run on foot
*“she broke the record for the half-mile run”*

<a id="entry-r-003404"></a>
## running

**發音：** /ˈɹʌnɪŋ/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **跑** — The action of the verb to run.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **賽跑 /賽跑** — The activity of running as a form of exercise, as a sport, or for any other reason.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **流量** — That which runs or flows; the quantity of a liquid which flows in a certain time or during a certain operation.
4. **運轉** — the state of being in operation
*“the engine is running smoothly”*
5. **賽跑** — the act of participating in an athletic competition involving running on a track
6. (American football) a play in which a player attempts to carry the ball through or past the opposing team
*“the defensive line braced to stop the run”*
*“the coach put great emphasis on running”*
*adj.* 1. (of fluids) moving or issuing in a stream
*“as mountain stream with freely running water”*
*“hovels without running water”*
2. continually repeated over a period of time
*“a running joke among us”*
3. of advancing the ball by running
*“the team's running plays worked better than its pass plays”*
4. executed or initiated by running
*“running plays worked better than pass plays”*
*“took a running jump”*
5. measured lengthwise
*“cost of lumber per running foot”*
6. (of e.g. a machine) performing or capable of performing
*“in running (or working) order”*
*“a functional set of brakes”*

## rush

**發音：** /ɹʌʃ/
*v.* 1. **快進, 趕快, 趕緊, 飛馳, 衝進** — move fast
*“He rushed down the hall to receive his guests”*
*“The cars raced down the street”*
2. *(intransitive, transitive)* **急速去, 急速做, 趕來 /趕來** — To hurry; to perform a task with great haste, often not properly or without thinking carefully.
3. **帶球衝向對方, 帶球跑** — run with the ball, in football
4. **使急速移動** — cause to move fast or to rush or race
*“The psychologist raced the rats through a long maze”*
5. *(transitive)* **突襲 /突襲** — To swiftly attack without warning.
6. attack suddenly
*n.* 1. **匆促, 匆促行事, 慌忙, 趕緊, 倉促** — the act of moving hurriedly and in a careless manner
*“in his haste to leave he forgot his book”*
2. **猛衝** — A sudden forward motion.
3. **燈芯草** — The stem of such plants used in making baskets, mats, the seats of chairs, etc.
4. a sudden forceful flow
5. grasslike plants growing in wet places and having cylindrical often hollow stems
6. physician and American Revolutionary leader; signer of the Declaration of Independence (1745-1813)
*adj.* 1. not accepting reservations
2. done under pressure
*“a rush job”*

## rust

*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **生鏽 /生鏽** — To oxidize, especially of iron or steel.
2. become destroyed by water, air, or a corrosive such as an acid

*“The acid corroded the metal”*
*“The steady dripping of water rusted the metal stopper in the sink”*
4. become coated with oxide
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **鏽 /鏽, 鐵鏽 /鐵鏽** — The deteriorated state of iron or steel as a result of moisture and oxidation; it consists mostly of iron(III) oxide (ferric oxide) and iron(II) oxide (ferrous oxide).
2. **生鏽, 類似鐵鏽的斑痕或外層, 鏽** — a red or brown oxide coating on iron or steel caused by the action of oxygen and moisture
3. **生鏽** — the formation of reddish-brown ferric oxides on iron by low-temperature oxidation in the presence of water
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **鏽病 /鏽病** — A disease of plants caused by a reddish-brown fungus (Pucciniales).
5. **鏽菌** — any of various fungi causing rust disease in plants
6. a plant disease that produces a reddish-brown discoloration of leaves and stems; caused by various rust fungi
*adj.* 1. **深褐色+的, 赭色+地, 鐵鏽色+的, 紅褐色+的** — of the brown color of rust

<a id="entry-r-003488"></a>

*adj.* 1. **村野+的, 鄉土氣+的, 鄉下+的** — characteristic of rural life
*“countrified clothes”*
*“rustic awkwardness”*
2. **鄉土+的, 鄉土氣+的, 農村+的, 鄉間+的** — characteristic of the fields or country
*“agrestic simplicity”*

3. awkwardly simple and provincial
*“bumpkinly country boys”*
*“rustic farmers”*

2. **使去鄉下** — send to the country

3. **罰暫時停學** — suspend temporarily from college or university, in England
4. give (stone) a rustic look
5. lend a rustic character to
*“rusticate the house in the country”*

<a id="entry-r-003496"></a>
